Cannot be combined with additional percentage vouchers.',\n53:   },\n54:   {\n55:     id: 'audi-q7-offer',\n56:     name: '2026 Audi Q7 Quattro',\n57:     category: 'Luxury SUV',\n58:     dailyPrice: 160,\n59:     discountPercent: 10,\n60:     promoCode: 'STUDENT10',\n61:     image:\n62:       'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1606664515524-ed2f786a0bd6?auto=format&fit=crop&w=900&q=85',\n63:     fallbackImage: buildFallbackCarImage('2026 Audi Q7 Quattro'),\n64:     alt: '2026 Audi Q7 Quattro luxury SUV in studio lighting',\n65:     specs: ['All-Wheel Drive', '7 Seats', 'Panoramic Roof', 'GPS Included'],\n66:     summary:\n67:       'Spacious three-row luxury SUV built for family road trips and campus travel, offered at 10% off the standard daily rate.',\n68:     inclusions: [\n69:       'All-weather Quattro drivetrain and built-in navigation',\n70:       'Flexible 24-hour free cancellation prior to pickup',\n71:       'Roadside assistance included 24/7',\n72:     ],\n73:     terms:\n74:       'Available for drivers aged 21+ with valid driver license and booking confirmation. Discount applies to base daily rental rate; optional child seats and toll passes billed separately.',\n75:   },\n76:   {\n77:     id: 'porsche-911-offer',\n78:     name: '2026 Porsche 911 Carrera',\n79:     category: 'Performance Coupe',\n80:     dailyPrice: 250,\n81:     discountPercent: 20,\n82:     promoCode: 'WEEKEND20',\n83:     image:\n84:       'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1503376780353-7e6692767b70?auto=format&fit=crop&w=900&q=85',\n85:     fallbackImage: buildFallbackCarImage('2026 Porsche 911 Carrera'),\n86:     alt: '2026 Porsche 911 Carrera sports coupe parked outdoors',\n87:     specs: ['8-Speed PDK', '2+2 Seats', 'Twin-Turbo Flat-6', 'Sport Chrono'],\n88:     summary:\n89:       'Signature grand touring performance with crisp handling and 20% weekend escape savings on multi-day bookings.',\n90:     inclusions: [\n91:       '250 daily miles included with discounted extra-mile tier',\n92:       'Dedicated concierge handover at CarVault flagship hubs',\n93:       'Full tank departure policy with transparent refueling',\n94:     ],\n95:     terms:\n96:       'Minimum driver age of 25 required for performance tier vehicles. Weekend 20% discount applies automatically to Friday-through-Monday reservations or with code WEEKEND20.',\n97:   },\n98: ];\n99: \n100: const PROMO_CODES = {\n101:   VAULT15: {\n102:     discount: 15,\n103:     description: '15% off Executive Sedan & Fleet Rentals',\n104:   },\n105:   STUDENT10: {\n106:     discount: 10,\n107:     description: '10% off Student & Campus Verified Rentals',\n108:   },\n109:   WEEKEND20: {\n110:     discount: 20,\n111:     description: '20% off Weekend & Performance Rentals',\n112:   },\n113: };\n114: \n115: const PROMO_STORAGE_KEY = 'carvault_active_promo';\n116: \n117: function formatCurrency(amount) {\n118:   return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', {\n119:     style: 'currency',\n120:     currency: 'USD',\n121:     minimumFractionDigits: 2,\n122:     maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n123:   }).format(amount);\n124: }\n125: \n126: function calculateDiscountedPrice(price, discountPercent) {\n127:   return price * (1 - discountPercent / 100);\n128: }\n129: \n130: function escapeHtml(text) {\n131:   const div = document.createElement('div');\n132:   div.textContent = String(text);\n133:   return div.innerHTML;\n134: }\n135: \n136: /**\n137:  * Attaches a safe one-time fallback image handler when an external URL fails.\n138:  */\n139: function attachImageFallback(imgElement, fallbackSrc) {\n140:   if (!imgElement || !fallbackSrc) return;\n141:   imgElement.onerror = function handleImgError() {\n142:     this.onerror = null;\n143:     this.src = fallbackSrc;\n144:   };\n145: }\n146: \n147: /**\n148:  * Mobile menu toggle using .navlinks.open and .menu-toggle.\n149:  */\n150: function initMobileNav() {\n151:   const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('menuToggle');\n152:   const navLinks = document.getElementById('navLinks');\n153:   if (!toggleBtn || !navLinks) return;\n154: \n155:   toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {\n156:     const isOpen = navLinks.classList.toggle('open');\n157:     toggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(isOpen));\n158:   });\n159: }\n160: \n161: /**\n162:  * Renders the 3 Special Vehicle Offer cards into #offers-grid using .card structure.\n163:  */\n164: function renderOfferCards(onViewOffer) {\n165:   const grid = document.getElementById('offers-grid');\n166:   if (!grid) return;\n167: \n168:   grid.innerHTML = OFFER_VEHICLES.map((offer) => {\n169:     const discountedPrice = calculateDiscountedPrice(offer.dailyPrice, offer.discountPercent);\n170:     const specsLine = offer.specs.join(' · ');\n171: \n172:     return `\n173:       <article class=\"card\" data-offer-id=\"${escapeHtml(offer.id)}\">\n174:         <div class=\"card-image\">\n175:           <img\n176:             src=\"${escapeHtml(offer.image)}\"\n177:             alt=\"${escapeHtml(offer.alt)}\"\n178:             loading=\"lazy\"\n179:             data-fallback=\"${escapeHtml(offer.fallbackImage)}\"\n180:           />\n181:           <span class=\"pill\" aria-label=\"${offer.discountPercent}% discount\">-${offer.discountPercent}%</span>\n182:         </div>\n183:         <div class=\"card-body\">\n184:           <div class=\"meta\">${escapeHtml(offer.category)} · ${escapeHtml(specsLine)}</div>\n185:           <h3>${escapeHtml(offer.name)}</h3>\n186:           <p>${escapeHtml(offer.summary)}</p>\n187:           <div class=\"card-bottom\">\n188:             <div>\n189:               <span class=\"muted\" style=\"text-decoration: line-through; margin-right: 8px; font-size: 13px;\">${formatCurrency(offer.dailyPrice)}</span>\n190:               <span class=\"price\">${formatCurrency(discountedPrice)}</span>\n191:               <span class=\"meta\">/ day</span>\n192:             </div>\n193:             <button\n194:               type=\"button\"\n195:               class=\"btn small js-view-offer\"\n196:               data-offer-id=\"${escapeHtml(offer.id)}\"\n197:             >\n198:               View\n199:             </button>\n200:           </div>\n201:         </div>\n202:       </article>\n203:     `;\n204:   }).join('');\n205: \n206:   grid.querySelectorAll('img[data-fallback]').forEach((img) => {\n207:     attachImageFallback(img, img.getAttribute('data-fallback'));\n208:   });\n209: \n210:   grid.addEventListener('click', (event) => {\n211:     const viewBtn = event.target.closest('.js-view-offer');\n212:     if (!viewBtn) return;\n213:     const offerId = viewBtn.getAttribute('data-offer-id');\n214:     onViewOffer(offerId);\n215:   });\n216: }\n217: \n218: /**\n219:  * Sets up the offer details modal using .panel and .hidden classes.\n220:  */\n221: function createOfferModalHandler() {\n222:   const modalEl = document.getElementById('offerDetailsModal');\n223:   const closeBtn = document.getElementById('offerModalClose');\n224:   const dismissBtn = document.getElementById('offerModalDismiss');\n225:   if (!modalEl) return () => {};\n226: \n227:   const titleEl = document.getElementById('offerModalTitle');\n228:   const categoryEl = document.getElementById('offerModalCategory');\n229:   const badgeEl = document.getElementById('offerModalBadge');\n230:   const imgEl = document.getElementById('offerModalImage');\n231:   const summaryEl = document.getElementById('offerModalSummary');\n232:   const originalPriceEl = document.getElementById('offerModalOriginalPrice');\n233:   const discountedPriceEl = document.getElementById('offerModalDiscountedPrice');\n234:   const savingsEl = document.getElementById('offerModalSavings');\n235:   const codeEl = document.getElementById('offerModalCode');\n236:   const specsEl = document.getElementById('offerModalSpecs');\n237:   const inclusionsEl = document.getElementById('offerModalInclusions');\n238:   const termsEl = document.getElementById('offerModalTerms');\n239: \n240:   function closeModal() {\n241:     modalEl.classList.add('hidden');\n242:     document.body.style.overflow = '';\n243:   }\n244: \n245:   closeBtn?.addEventListener('click', closeModal);\n246:   dismissBtn?.addEventListener('click', closeModal);\n247: \n248:   modalEl.addEventListener('click', (event) => {\n249:     if (event.target === modalEl) {\n250:       closeModal();\n251:     }\n252:   });\n253: \n254:   document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {\n255:     if (event.key === 'Escape' && !modalEl.classList.contains('hidden')) {\n256:       closeModal();\n257:     }\n258:   });\n259: \n260:   return function openOfferModal(offerId) {\n261:     const offer = OFFER_VEHICLES.find((item) => item.id === offerId);\n262:     if (!offer) return;\n263: \n264:     const discountedPrice = calculateDiscountedPrice(offer.dailyPrice, offer.discountPercent);\n265:     const dailySavings = offer.dailyPrice - discountedPrice;\n266: \n267:     titleEl.textContent = offer.name;\n268:     categoryEl.textContent = offer.category;\n269:     badgeEl.textContent = `-${offer.discountPercent}% OFF`;\n270:     imgEl.src = offer.image;\n271:     imgEl.alt = offer.alt;\n272:     attachImageFallback(imgEl, offer.fallbackImage);\n273:     summaryEl.textContent = offer.summary;\n274:     originalPriceEl.textContent = `${formatCurrency(offer.dailyPrice)} / day`;\n275:     discountedPriceEl.textContent = `${formatCurrency(discountedPrice)} / day`;\n276:     savingsEl.textContent = `Save ${formatCurrency(dailySavings)} per day`;\n277:     codeEl.textContent = offer.promoCode;\n278:     specsEl.textContent = offer.specs.join(' · ');\n279:     termsEl.textContent = offer.terms;\n280: \n281:     inclusionsEl.innerHTML = offer.inclusions\n282:       .map((item) => `<li>${escapeHtml(item)}</li>`)\n283:       .join('');\n284: \n285:     modalEl.classList.remove('hidden');\n286:     document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';\n287:     closeBtn?.focus();\n288:   };\n289: }\n290: \n291: /**\n292:  * Initializes the Discount Code Box with validation and localStorage persistence.\n293:  */\n294: function initPromoCodeBox() {\n295:   const form = document.getElementById('promo-code-form');\n296:   const input = document.getElementById('promo-code-input');\n297:   const feedbackEl = document.getElementById('promo-feedback');\n298:   if (!form || !input || !feedbackEl) return;\n299: \n300:   function renderFeedback(type, message, activeCode = '') {\n301:     feedbackEl.className = type === 'success' ? 'notice row' : 'notice error';\n302:     if (type === 'success' && activeCode) {\n303:       feedbackEl.innerHTML = `\n304:         <span>${escapeHtml(message)}</span>\n305:         <button type=\"button\" class=\"btn ghost small\" id=\"clear-promo-btn\">Remove</button>\n306:       `;\n307:       const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clear-promo-btn');\n308:       clearBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {\n309:         localStorage.removeItem(PROMO_STORAGE_KEY);\n310:         input.value = '';\n311:         feedbackEl.className = 'notice hidden';\n312:         feedbackEl.innerHTML = '';\n313:         input.focus();\n314:       });\n315:     } else {\n316:       feedbackEl.textContent = message;\n317:     }\n318:   }\n319: \n320:   function applyPromoCode(rawCode) {\n321:     const normalizedCode = rawCode.trim().toUpperCase();\n322: \n323:     if (!normalizedCode) {\n324:       renderFeedback('error', 'Please enter a promo code before applying.');\n325:       return;\n326:     }\n327: \n328:     const promo = PROMO_CODES[normalizedCode];\n329:     if (!promo) {\n330:       renderFeedback(\n331:         'error',\n332:         `Promo code \"${normalizedCode}\" is invalid. Try VAULT15, STUDENT10, or WEEKEND20.`\n333:       );\n334:       return;\n335:     }\n336: \n337:     localStorage.setItem(PROMO_STORAGE_KEY, normalizedCode);\n338:     input.value = normalizedCode;\n339:     renderFeedback(\n340:       'success',\n341:       `Applied ${normalizedCode}: ${promo.description} (saved for your session).`,\n342:       normalizedCode\n343:     );\n344:   }\n345: \n346:   const savedCode = (localStorage.getItem(PROMO_STORAGE_KEY) || '').trim().toUpperCase();\n347:   if (savedCode && PROMO_CODES[savedCode]) {\n348:     const promo = PROMO_CODES[savedCode];\n349:     input.value = savedCode;\n350:     renderFeedback(\n351:       'success',\n352:       `Active code ${savedCode}: ${promo.description} (restored from saved session).`,\n353:       savedCode\n354:     );\n355:   }\n356: \n357:   form.addEventListener('submit', (event) => {\n358:     event.preventDefault();\n359:     applyPromoCode(input.value);\n360:   });\n361: }\n362: \n363: /**\n364:  * Initializes the real-time Discount Calculator Box.\n365:  */\n366: function initDiscountCalculator() {\n367:   const priceInput = document.getElementById('calc-price');\n368:   const discountInput = document.getElementById('calc-discount');\n369:   const totalEl = document.getElementById('calc-total');\n370:   const savingsEl = document.getElementById('calc-savings');\n371:   const feedbackEl = document.getElementById('calc-feedback');\n372:   if (!priceInput || !discountInput || !totalEl || !savingsEl || !feedbackEl) return;\n373: \n374:   function setError(message) {\n375:     feedbackEl.className = 'notice error';\n376:     feedbackEl.textContent = message;\n377:     totalEl.textContent = formatCurrency(0);\n378:     savingsEl.textContent = `Savings: ${formatCurrency(0)}`;\n379:   }\n380: \n381:   function updateCalculation() {\n382:     const rawPrice = priceInput.value.trim();\n383:     const rawDiscount = discountInput.value.trim();\n384: \n385:     if (priceInput.validity?.badInput) {\n386:       setError('Price must be a valid non-negative amount.');\n387:       return;\n388:     }\n389: \n390:     if (discountInput.validity?.badInput) {\n391:       setError('Discount percentage must be between 0% and 100%.');\n392:       return;\n393:     }\n394: \n395:     if (rawPrice === '' && rawDiscount === '') {\n396:       feedbackEl.className = 'meta';\n397:       feedbackEl.style.marginTop = '12px';\n398:       feedbackEl.textContent =\n399:         'Enter a rental price and discount percentage to see your live total.';\n400:       totalEl.textContent = formatCurrency(0);\n401:       savingsEl.textContent = `Savings: ${formatCurrency(0)}`;\n402:       return;\n403:     }\n404: \n405:     const price = rawPrice === '' ? NaN : Number(rawPrice);\n406:     const discount = rawDiscount === '' ? 0 : Number(rawDiscount);\n407: \n408:     if (rawPrice === '') {\n409:       feedbackEl.className = 'meta';\n410:       feedbackEl.style.marginTop = '12px';\n411:       feedbackEl.textContent = 'Enter a rental price to calculate your discounted total.';\n412:       totalEl.textContent = formatCurrency(0);\n413:       savingsEl.textContent = `Savings: ${formatCurrency(0)}`;\n414:       return;\n415:     }\n416: \n417:     if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price < 0) {\n418:       setError('Price must be a valid non-negative amount.');\n419:       return;\n420:     }\n421: \n422:     if (rawDiscount !== '' && (!Number.isFinite(discount) || discount < 0 || discount > 100)) {\n423:       setError('Discount percentage must be between 0% and 100%.');\n424:       return;\n425:     }\n426: \n427:     const savings = price * (discount / 100);\n428:     const finalTotal = Math.max(0, price - savings);\n429: \n430:     totalEl.textContent = formatCurrency(finalTotal);\n431:     savingsEl.textContent = `Savings: ${formatCurrency(savings)}`;\n432:     feedbackEl.className = 'meta';\n433:     feedbackEl.style.marginTop = '12px';\n434:     feedbackEl.textContent =\n435:       discount > 0\n436:         ? `${discount}% discount applied to ${formatCurrency(price)}.`\n437:         : 'Showing standard rate (0% discount).';\n438:   }\n439: \n440:   priceInput.addEventListener('input', updateCalculation);\n441:   discountInput.addEventListener('input', updateCalculation);\n442:   updateCalculation();\n443: }\n444: \n445: document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {\n446:   initMobileNav();\n447:   const openOfferModal = createOfferModalHandler();\n448:   renderOfferCards(openOfferModal);\n449:   initPromoCodeBox();\n450:   initDiscountCalculator();\n451: });"}

{"step_index":12,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T15:32:16Z","model":"claude-sonnet-4-6","input_tokens":210,"cache_read_tokens":31295,"output_tokens":8868,"tool_calls":[{"name":"write_file","args":{"Content":"/* ============================================================\n   CarVault — Offers Page Theme\n   Layered on top of css/style.css\n   Brand palette: dark obsidian header + crisp white body\n   ============================================================ */\n\n/* ── 1. DESIGN TOKENS ───────────────────────────────────────── */\n:root {\n  /* Topbar / Header */\n  --ov-topbar-bg   : #1d1f21;\n  --ov-header-bg   : #141517;\n\n  /* Gold */\n  --ov-gold        : #eab308;\n  --ov-gold-hover  : #fbbf24;\n\n  /* Body (white theme) */\n  --ov-body-bg     : #ffffff;\n  --ov-body-alt    : #f8f9fa;\n  --ov-text        : #111315;\n  --ov-muted       : #5a6372;\n  --ov-border      : #e2e6ec;\n\n  /* Hero (dark charcoal) */\n  --ov-hero-from   : #16181b;\n  --ov-hero-to     : #1d1f23;\n\n  /* Footer */\n  --ov-footer-bg   : #141517;\n  --ov-footer-line : #2a2c30;\n}\n\n/* ── 2. BASE BODY RESET for white theme ─────────────────────── */\nbody {\n  background : var(--ov-body-bg);\n  color      : var(--ov-text);\n}\n\n/* ── 3. TOPBAR ───────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n.topbar {\n  background    : var(--ov-topbar-bg) !important;\n  color         : #9ca3af !important;\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  letter-spacing: .18em;\n  padding       : 9px 16px;\n  text-align    : center;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  border-bottom : 1px solid #2a2c30;\n}\n\n/* ── 4. HEADER & NAV ─────────────────────────────────────────── */\n.header {\n  position      : sticky;\n  top           : 0;\n  z-index       : 50;\n  background    : var(--ov-header-bg) !important;\n  backdrop-filter: none;\n  border-bottom : 1px solid #2a2c30;\n}\n\n/* Logo */\n.ov-logo {\n  font-family   : 'Plus Jakarta Sans', 'Manrope', sans-serif;\n  font-size     : 20px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  letter-spacing: .18em;\n  color         : #f1f5f9;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  display       : inline-flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  gap           : 2px;\n  white-space   : nowrap;\n}\n.ov-logo .ov-star {\n  color : var(--ov-gold);\n  font-size: 14px;\n  margin: 0 3px;\n  line-height: 1;\n}\n\n/* Nav links */\n.navlinks a {\n  color         : #cbd5e1 !important;\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  letter-spacing: .11em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  transition    : color .18s;\n}\n.navlinks a:hover,\n.navlinks a.active {\n  color : var(--ov-gold) !important;\n}\n\n/* CTA pill button */\n.ov-cta-btn {\n  display       : inline-flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  gap           : 6px;\n  padding       : 10px 20px;\n  background    : var(--ov-gold);\n  color         : #111315 !important;\n  border        : none;\n  border-radius : 999px;\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  letter-spacing: .09em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  white-space   : nowrap;\n  transition    : background .18s, transform .18s;\n  cursor        : pointer;\n}\n.ov-cta-btn:hover {\n  background : var(--ov-gold-hover);\n  transform  : translateY(-2px);\n  color      : #111315 !important;\n}\n\n/* Mobile menu toggle */\n#menuToggle {\n  background    : transparent;\n  border        : 1px solid #3a3d42;\n  color         : #cbd5e1;\n  border-radius : 6px;\n  padding       : 8px 14px;\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  letter-spacing: .1em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n}\n#menuToggle:hover {\n  border-color  : var(--ov-gold);\n  color         : var(--ov-gold);\n}\n\n@media (max-width: 960px) {\n  .navlinks {\n    background    : #1a1c1f !important;\n    border-bottom : 1px solid #2a2c30 !important;\n    top           : 70px !important;\n  }\n}\n\n/* ── 5. HERO (dark charcoal) ─────────────────────────────────── */\n.ov-hero {\n  min-height    : 480px;\n  display       : flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  background    : linear-gradient(135deg, var(--ov-hero-from) 0%, var(--ov-hero-to) 100%);\n  padding       : 90px 0 80px;\n  position      : relative;\n  overflow      : hidden;\n}\n.ov-hero::before {\n  content    : '';\n  position   : absolute;\n  inset      : 0;\n  background : radial-gradient(ellipse at 80% 30%, rgba(234,179,8,.07) 0%, transparent 65%),\n               radial-gradient(ellipse at 10% 70%, rgba(255,255,255,.03) 0%, transparent 55%);\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n.ov-hero .wrap { position: relative; z-index: 1; }\n\n.ov-hero-eyebrow {\n  color         : var(--ov-gold);\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  letter-spacing: .3em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  margin-bottom : 18px;\n}\n.ov-hero h1 {\n  font-family   : 'Plus Jakarta Sans', 'Manrope', sans-serif;\n  font-size     : clamp(2.6rem, 5vw, 4.4rem);\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  color         : #f1f5f9;\n  letter-spacing: -.045em;\n  line-height   : 1.08;\n  margin-bottom : 18px;\n  max-width     : 680px;\n}\n.ov-hero p {\n  color         : #9ca3af;\n  font-size     : 16px;\n  max-width     : 520px;\n  margin-bottom : 34px;\n  line-height   : 1.7;\n}\n.ov-hero-actions {\n  display       : flex;\n  gap           : 12px;\n  flex-wrap     : wrap;\n}\n.ov-hero-btn-primary {\n  display       : inline-flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  gap           : 7px;\n  padding       : 13px 26px;\n  background    : var(--ov-gold);\n  color         : #111315 !important;\n  border-radius : 8px;\n  font-size     : 12px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  letter-spacing: .08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  transition    : background .18s, transform .18s;\n  border        : none;\n}\n.ov-hero-btn-primary:hover {\n  background : var(--ov-gold-hover);\n  transform  : translateY(-2px);\n}\n.ov-hero-btn-outline {\n  display       : inline-flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  gap           : 7px;\n  padding       : 13px 26px;\n  background    : transparent;\n  color         : #f1f5f9 !important;\n  border        : 1px solid #3a3d42;\n  border-radius : 8px;\n  font-size     : 12px;\n  font-weight   : 700;\n  letter-spacing: .08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  transition    : border-color .18s, color .18s;\n}\n.ov-hero-btn-outline:hover {\n  border-color : var(--ov-gold);\n  color        : var(--ov-gold) !important;\n}\n\n/* ── 6. MAIN BODY — white sections ───────────────────────────── */\nmain {\n  background : var(--ov-body-bg);\n}\n\n/* Regular sections */\n.ov-section {\n  padding    : 80px 0;\n  background : var(--ov-body-bg);\n}\n.ov-section-alt {\n  padding    : 80px 0;\n  background : var(--ov-body-alt);\n}\n\n/* Section header */\n.ov-section-head {\n  display        : flex;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  align-items    : flex-end;\n  gap            : 24px;\n  margin-bottom  : 36px;\n}\n.ov-section-head p {\n  max-width : 440px;\n  color     : var(--ov-muted);\n  font-size : 14px;\n  line-height: 1.65;\n  margin    : 0;\n}\n\n.ov-eyebrow {\n  color         : var(--ov-gold);\n  font-size     : 10px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  letter-spacing: .28em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  margin-bottom : 10px;\n}\n.ov-heading {\n  font-family   : 'Plus Jakarta Sans', 'Manrope', sans-serif;\n  font-size     : clamp(1.6rem, 2.8vw, 2.4rem);\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  color         : var(--ov-text);\n  letter-spacing: -.03em;\n  margin        : 0;\n}\n\n/* ── 7. OFFER CARDS (white body) ─────────────────────────────── */\n.ov-grid {\n  display              : grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));\n  gap                  : 24px;\n}\n\n.ov-card {\n  background    : #ffffff;\n  border        : 1px solid var(--ov-border);\n  border-radius : 12px;\n  overflow      : hidden;\n  transition    : border-color .22s, box-shadow .22s, transform .22s;\n}\n.ov-card:hover {\n  border-color : var(--ov-gold);\n  box-shadow   : 0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.10);\n  transform    : translateY(-3px);\n}\n\n.ov-card-img-wrap {\n  height     : 230px;\n  position   : relative;\n  overflow   : hidden;\n  background : #f1f3f5;\n}\n.ov-card-img-wrap img {\n  width      : 100%;\n  height     : 100%;\n  object-fit : cover;\n  display    : block;\n  transition : transform .45s;\n}\n.ov-card:hover .ov-card-img-wrap img {\n  transform  : scale(1.045);\n}\n\n.ov-badge {\n  position      : absolute;\n  top           : 14px;\n  left          : 14px;\n  padding       : 5px 10px;\n  background    : var(--ov-gold);\n  color         : #111315;\n  font-size     : 10px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  letter-spacing: .09em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  border-radius : 4px;\n}\n\n.ov-card-body {\n  padding : 22px 24px 24px;\n}\n.ov-card-category {\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  font-weight   : 700;\n  color         : var(--ov-muted);\n  letter-spacing: .07em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  margin-bottom : 6px;\n}\n.ov-card-name {\n  font-family   : 'Plus Jakarta Sans', 'Manrope', sans-serif;\n  font-size     : 18px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  color         : var(--ov-text);\n  margin        : 0 0 8px;\n  letter-spacing: -.02em;\n}\n.ov-card-summary {\n  font-size   : 13px;\n  color       : var(--ov-muted);\n  line-height : 1.6;\n  margin      : 0 0 16px;\n}\n.ov-card-code {\n  display       : inline-flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  gap           : 6px;\n  background    : #fefce8;\n  border        : 1px solid #fde68a;\n  border-radius : 6px;\n  padding       : 4px 10px;\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  font-weight   : 700;\n  color         : #92400e;\n  letter-spacing: .06em;\n  margin-bottom : 18px;\n}\n\n.ov-card-footer {\n  display        : flex;\n  align-items    : center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  padding-top    : 16px;\n  border-top     : 1px solid var(--ov-border);\n  gap            : 10px;\n}\n.ov-price-block {\n  display    : flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap        : 2px;\n}\n.ov-price-original {\n  font-size       : 12px;\n  color           : var(--ov-muted);\n  text-decoration : line-through;\n}\n.ov-price-sale {\n  font-size   : 22px;\n  font-weight : 800;\n  color       : var(--ov-text);\n  line-height : 1;\n}\n.ov-price-sale span {\n  font-size   : 12px;\n  font-weight : 500;\n  color       : var(--ov-muted);\n}\n.ov-view-btn {\n  display       : inline-flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  gap           : 5px;\n  padding       : 10px 18px;\n  background    : var(--ov-gold);\n  color         : #111315;\n  border        : none;\n  border-radius : 8px;\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  letter-spacing: .08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  cursor        : pointer;\n  transition    : background .18s, transform .18s;\n  white-space   : nowrap;\n}\n.ov-view-btn:hover {\n  background : var(--ov-gold-hover);\n  transform  : translateY(-1px);\n}\n\n/* ── 8. TOOLS SECTION (promo + calculator) ───────────────────── */\n.ov-tools-grid {\n  display              : grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));\n  gap                  : 24px;\n}\n\n.ov-panel {\n  background    : #ffffff;\n  border        : 1px solid var(--ov-border);\n  border-radius : 12px;\n  padding       : 32px;\n}\n.ov-panel h3 {\n  font-family   : 'Plus Jakarta Sans', 'Manrope', sans-serif;\n  font-size     : 18px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  color         : var(--ov-text);\n  margin        : 0 0 8px;\n}\n.ov-panel > p {\n  font-size   : 13px;\n  color       : var(--ov-muted);\n  line-height : 1.6;\n  margin      : 0 0 22px;\n}\n\nlabel.ov-label {\n  display       : block;\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  font-weight   : 700;\n  color         : #374151;\n  letter-spacing: .06em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  margin-bottom : 8px;\n}\n\n.ov-input {\n  width         : 100%;\n  background    : #f9fafb;\n  color         : var(--ov-text);\n  border        : 1px solid var(--ov-border);\n  border-radius : 8px;\n  padding       : 12px 15px;\n  font-size     : 14px;\n  outline       : none;\n  transition    : border-color .18s, box-shadow .18s;\n}\n.ov-input:focus {\n  border-color : var(--ov-gold);\n  box-shadow   : 0 0 0 3px rgba(234,179,8,.15);\n}\n\n.ov-input-row {\n  display : flex;\n  gap     : 10px;\n  align-items: flex-end;\n}\n.ov-input-row .ov-input { flex: 1; }\n\n.ov-apply-btn {\n  flex-shrink   : 0;\n  padding       : 12px 20px;\n  background    : var(--ov-gold);\n  color         : #111315;\n  border        : none;\n  border-radius : 8px;\n  font-size     : 12px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  letter-spacing: .08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  cursor        : pointer;\n  transition    : background .18s;\n  white-space   : nowrap;\n}\n.ov-apply-btn:hover { background: var(--ov-gold-hover); }\n\n.ov-active-codes {\n  margin-top  : 16px;\n  font-size   : 12px;\n  color       : var(--ov-muted);\n}\n.ov-active-codes .ov-code-chip {\n  display       : inline-flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  gap           : 3px;\n  background    : #fefce8;\n  border        : 1px solid #fde68a;\n  border-radius : 4px;\n  padding       : 2px 7px;\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  font-weight   : 700;\n  color         : #92400e;\n  margin        : 0 3px;\n}\n\n/* Promo feedback notices */\n.ov-notice {\n  margin-top    : 14px;\n  padding       : 12px 16px;\n  border-radius : 8px;\n  font-size     : 13px;\n  line-height   : 1.5;\n  display       : flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap           : 10px;\n}\n.ov-notice.success {\n  background : #f0fdf4;\n  border     : 1px solid #bbf7d0;\n  color      : #166534;\n}\n.ov-notice.error {\n  background : #fef2f2;\n  border     : 1px solid #fecaca;\n  color      : #991b1b;\n}\n.ov-notice-remove {\n  flex-shrink   : 0;\n  padding       : 4px 10px;\n  background    : transparent;\n  border        : 1px solid currentColor;\n  border-radius : 6px;\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  font-weight   : 700;\n  cursor        : pointer;\n  color         : inherit;\n  opacity       : .75;\n  transition    : opacity .15s;\n}\n.ov-notice-remove:hover { opacity: 1; }\n\n/* Calculator two-col inputs */\n.ov-calc-grid {\n  display              : grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;\n  gap                  : 16px;\n  margin-bottom        : 16px;\n}\n.ov-calc-hint {\n  font-size  : 12px;\n  color      : var(--ov-muted);\n  margin-top : 10px;\n}\n\n.ov-stat-box {\n  margin-top    : 20px;\n  padding       : 20px 22px;\n  background    : #f9fafb;\n  border        : 1px solid var(--ov-border);\n  border-radius : 10px;\n  border-top    : 3px solid var(--ov-gold);\n}\n.ov-stat-label {\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  font-weight   : 700;\n  color         : var(--ov-muted);\n  letter-spacing: .1em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  margin-bottom : 6px;\n}\n.ov-stat-value {\n  font-size   : 32px;\n  font-weight : 800;\n  color       : var(--ov-text);\n  line-height : 1;\n  margin-bottom: 4px;\n}\n.ov-stat-savings {\n  font-size   : 13px;\n  color       : #16a34a;\n  font-weight : 700;\n}\n\n/* ── 9. MODAL (dark glass over white page) ───────────────────── */\n.ov-modal-backdrop {\n  position      : fixed;\n  inset         : 0;\n  z-index       : 90;\n  background    : rgba(10, 12, 14, 0.80);\n  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);\n  display       : flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  padding       : 20px;\n}\n.ov-modal-dialog {\n  width         : min(760px, 100%);\n  max-height    : 90vh;\n  overflow-y    : auto;\n  background    : #ffffff;\n  border        : 1px solid var(--ov-border);\n  border-radius : 16px;\n  box-shadow    : 0 24px 72px rgba(0,0,0,.35);\n  padding       : 32px;\n}\n.ov-modal-header {\n  display        : flex;\n  align-items    : flex-start;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap            : 16px;\n  margin-bottom  : 20px;\n}\n.ov-modal-category {\n  font-size     : 10px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  letter-spacing: .2em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  color         : var(--ov-gold);\n  margin-bottom : 5px;\n}\n.ov-modal-title {\n  font-family   : 'Plus Jakarta Sans', 'Manrope', sans-serif;\n  font-size     : 22px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  color         : var(--ov-text);\n  margin        : 0;\n}\n.ov-modal-close-btn {\n  flex-shrink   : 0;\n  padding       : 8px 14px;\n  background    : #f3f4f6;\n  border        : 1px solid var(--ov-border);\n  border-radius : 8px;\n  font-size     : 12px;\n  font-weight   : 700;\n  color         : var(--ov-muted);\n  cursor        : pointer;\n  transition    : border-color .15s, color .15s;\n}\n.ov-modal-close-btn:hover {\n  border-color : var(--ov-gold);\n  color        : var(--ov-text);\n}\n.ov-modal-img {\n  width         : 100%;\n  height        : 240px;\n  object-fit    : cover;\n  border-radius : 10px;\n  border        : 1px solid var(--ov-border);\n  margin-bottom : 20px;\n  display       : block;\n}\n.ov-modal-badges {\n  display     : flex;\n  align-items : center;\n  gap         : 12px;\n  flex-wrap   : wrap;\n  margin-bottom: 14px;\n}\n.ov-modal-badge {\n  display       : inline-flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  padding       : 5px 11px;\n  background    : var(--ov-gold);\n  color         : #111315;\n  border-radius : 6px;\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  font-weight   : 800;\n  letter-spacing: .07em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n}\n.ov-modal-code-tag {\n  display       : inline-flex;\n  align-items   : center;\n  gap           : 5px;\n  background    : #fefce8;\n  border        : 1px solid #fde68a;\n  border-radius : 6px;\n  padding       : 5px 11px;\n  font-size     : 11px;\n  font-weight   : .08em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  cursor        : pointer;\n  transition    : background .18s;\n}\n.ov-modal-reserve:hover { background: var(--ov-gold-hover); }\n\n/* FALLBACK SVG BUILDER ──────────────────────────────── */\nfunction buildFallbackCarImage(label) {\n  const svg = `\n    <svg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" viewBox=\"0 0 900 540\" role=\"img\" aria-label=\"${label}\">\n      <defs>\n        <linearGradient id=\"cvbg\" x1=\"0\" y1=\"0\" x2=\"1\" y2=\"1\">\n          <stop offset=\"0%\" stop-color=\"#16181b\"/>\n          <stop offset=\"100%\" stop-color=\"#1d1f23\"/>\n        </linearGradient>\n      </defs>\n      <rect width=\"900\" height=\"540\" fill=\"url(#cvbg)\"/>\n      <circle cx=\"720\" cy=\"110\" r=\"160\" fill=\"#eab308\" opacity=\"0.08\"/>\n      <path d=\"M180 340 L240 250 Q285 200 375 198 L545 198 Q620 202 675 254 L760 272 Q780 286 774 340 Z\"\n            fill=\"#23262a\" stroke=\"#eab308\" stroke-width=\"3\"/>\n      <circle cx=\"305\" cy=\"342\" r=\"44\" fill=\"#16181b\" stroke=\"#eab308\" stroke-width=\"5\"/>\n      <circle cx=\"620\" cy=\"342\" r=\"44\" fill=\"#16181b\" stroke=\"#eab308\" stroke-width=\"5\"/>\n      <text x=\"56\" y=\"455\" fill=\"#f1f5f9\" font-family=\"Manrope,sans-serif\" font-size=\"30\" font-weight=\"800\">${label}</text>\n      <text x=\"56\" y=\"490\" fill=\"#9ca3af\" font-family=\"DM Sans,sans-serif\" font-size=\"18\">CarVault Seasonal Privileges</text>\n    </svg>\n  `.trim();\n  return `data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,${encodeURIComponent(svg)}`;\n}\n\n/* ── 2. OFFER DATA ────────────────────────────────────────── */\nconst OFFER_VEHICLES = [\n  {\n    id              : 'bmw-530i-offer',\n    name            : '2026 BMW 530i M Sport',\n    category        : 'Executive Sedan',\n    originalPrice   : 68500,\n    discountPercent : 15,\n    finalPrice      : 58225,\n    promoCode       : 'VAULT15',\n    image           : 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1555215695-3004980ad54e?auto=format&fit=crop&w=900&q=85',\n    alt             : '2026 BMW 530i M Sport executive sedan on open road',\n    specs           : ['Automatic', '5 Seats', 'Hybrid Petrol', 'Unlimited Mileage'],\n    summary         : 'Refined business-class comfort with adaptive suspension, wireless cockpit integration, and 15% seasonal fleet savings.',\n    inclusions      : [\n      'Comprehensive collision damage waiver included',\n      'Unlimited daily mileage across city and highway routes',\n      'Complimentary second driver registration',\n    ],\n    terms           : 'Valid on orders of 2 days or longer booked through CarVault. Standard security deposit applies at pickup. Cannot be combined with additional percentage vouchers.',\n  },\n  {\n    id              : 'audi-q7-offer',\n    name            : '2026 Audi Q7 Quattro',\n    category        : 'Luxury SUV',\n    originalPrice   : 74000,\n    discountPercent : 10,\n    finalPrice      : 66600,\n    promoCode       : 'STUDENT10',\n    image           : 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1606664515524-ed2f786a0bd6?auto=format&fit=crop&w=900&q=85',\n    alt             : '2026 Audi Q7 Quattro luxury SUV in studio lighting',\n    specs           : ['All-Wheel Drive', '7 Seats', 'Panoramic Roof', 'GPS Included'],\n    summary         : 'Spacious three-row luxury SUV built for family road trips and campus travel, offered at 10% off the standard rate.',\n    inclusions      : [\n      'All-weather Quattro drivetrain and built-in navigation',\n      'Flexible 24-hour free cancellation prior to pickup',\n      'Roadside assistance included 24/7',\n    ],\n    terms           : 'Available for drivers aged 21+ with valid driver license and booking confirmation. Discount applies to base rate; optional add-ons billed separately.',\n  },\n  {\n    id              : 'porsche-911-offer',\n    name            : '2026 Porsche 911 Carrera',\n    category        : 'Performance Coupe',\n    originalPrice   : 122000,\n    discountPercent : 20,\n    finalPrice      : 97600,\n    promoCode       : 'WEEKEND20',\n    image           : 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1503376780353-7e6692767b70?auto=format&fit=crop&w=900&q=85',\n    alt             : '2026 Porsche 911 Carrera sports coupe parked outdoors',\n    specs           : ['8-Speed PDK', '2+2 Seats', 'Twin-Turbo Flat-6', 'Sport Chrono'],\n    summary         : 'Signature grand touring performance with crisp handling and 20% weekend escape savings on multi-day orders.',\n    inclusions      : [\n      '250 daily miles included with discounted extra-mile tier',\n      'Dedicated concierge handover at CarVault flagship hubs',\n      'Full tank departure policy with transparent refueling',\n    ],\n    terms           : 'Minimum driver age of 25 required for performance tier vehicles. Weekend 20% discount applies automatically to Friday-through-Monday reservations or with code WEEKEND20.',\n  },\n];\n\nconst PROMO_CODES = {\n  VAULT15  : { discount: 15, description: '15% off Executive Sedan & Fleet Orders'       },\n  STUDENT10: { discount: 10, description: '10% off Student & Campus Verified Orders'      },\n  WEEKEND20: { discount: 20, description: '20% off Weekend & Performance Orders'          },\n};\n\nconst PROMO_STORAGE_KEY = 'carvault_active_promo';\n\n/* ── 3. HELPERS ───────────────────────────────────────────── */\nfunction formatCurrency(amount) {\n  return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', {\n    style               : 'currency',\n    currency            : 'USD',\n    minimumFractionDigits: 2,\n    maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n  }).format(amount);\n}\n\nfunction escapeHtml(str) {\n  const div = document.createElement('div');\n  div.textContent = String(str);\n  return div.innerHTML;\n}\n\nfunction attachImageFallback(imgEl, fallbackSrc) {\n  if (!imgEl || !fallbackSrc) return;\n  imgEl.onerror = function () {\n    this.onerror = null;\n    this.src = fallbackSrc;\n  };\n}\n\n/* ── 4. MOBILE NAV ────────────────────────────────────────── */\nfunction initMobileNav() {\n  const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('menuToggle');\n  const navLinks  = document.getElementById('navLinks');\n  if (!toggleBtn || !navLinks) return;\n  toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {\n    const isOpen = navLinks.classList.toggle('open');\n    toggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(isOpen));\n  });\n}\n\n/* ── 5. OFFER CARDS ───────────────────────────────────────── */\nfunction renderOfferCards(onViewOffer) {\n  const grid = document.getElementById('offers-grid');\n  if (!grid) return;\n\n  grid.innerHTML = OFFER_VEHICLES.map((offer) => {\n    const savings     = offer.originalPrice - offer.finalPrice;\n    const fallbackSrc = buildFallbackCarImage(offer.name);\n\n    return `\n      <article class=\"ov-card\" role=\"listitem\" data-offer-id=\"${escapeHtml(offer.id)}\">\n\n        <div class=\"ov-card-img-wrap\">\n          <img\n            src=\"${escapeHtml(offer.image)}\"\n            alt=\"${escapeHtml(offer.alt)}\"\n            loading=\"lazy\"\n            data-fallback=\"${escapeHtml(fallbackSrc)}\"\n          />\n          <span class=\"ov-badge\" aria-label=\"${offer.discountPercent}% discount\">\n            &minus;${offer.discountPercent}%\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"ov-card-body\">\n          <div class=\"ov-card-category\">${escapeHtml(offer.category)}</div>\n          <h3 class=\"ov-card-name\">${escapeHtml(offer.name)}</h3>\n          <p class=\"ov-card-summary\">${escapeHtml(offer.summary)}</p>\n\n          <div class=\"ov-card-code\">\n            <svg width=\"12\" height=\"12\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2.5\" aria-hidden=\"true\">\n              <rect x=\"3\" y=\"11\" width=\"18\" height=\"11\" rx=\"2\" ry=\"2\"/><path d=\"M7 11V7a5 5 0 0 1 10 0v4\"/>\n            </svg>\n            Code: <strong>${escapeHtml(offer.promoCode)}</strong>\n          </div>\n\n          <div class=\"ov-card-footer\">\n            <div class=\"ov-price-block\">\n              <span class=\"ov-price-original\">${formatCurrency(offer.originalPrice)}</span>\n              <span class=\"ov-price-sale\">\n                ${formatCurrency(offer.finalPrice)}<span> / save ${formatCurrency(savings)}</span>\n              </span>\n            </div>\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              class=\"ov-view-btn js-view-offer\"\n              data-offer-id=\"${escapeHtml(offer.id)}\"\n              aria-label=\"View details for ${escapeHtml(offer.name)}\"\n            >\n              View Details\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n      </article>\n    `;\n  }).join('');\n\n  /* Attach onerror fallbacks */\n  grid.querySelectorAll('img[data-fallback]').forEach((img) => {\n    attachImageFallback(img, img.getAttribute('data-fallback'));\n  });\n\n  /* Delegated click handler */\n  grid.addEventListener('click', (e) => {\n    const btn = e.target.closest('.js-view-offer');\n    if (!btn) return;\n    onViewOffer(btn.getAttribute('data-offer-id'));\n  });\n}\n\n/* ── 6. MODAL ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */\nfunction createOfferModalHandler() {\n  const modalEl        = document.getElementById('offerDetailsModal');\n  const closeBtn       = document.getElementById('offerModalClose');\n  const dismissBtn     = document.getElementById('offerModalDismiss');\n  if (!modalEl) return () => {};\n\n  const titleEl        = document.getElementById('offerModalTitle');\n  const categoryEl     = document.getElementById('offerModalCategory');\n  const badgeEl        = document.getElementById('offerModalBadge');\n  const imgEl          = document.getElementById('offerModalImage');\n  const summaryEl      = document.getElementById('offerModalSummary');\n  const originalPriceEl= document.getElementById('offerModalOriginalPrice');\n  const discountedPriceEl = document.getElementById('offerModalDiscountedPrice');\n  const savingsEl      = document.getElementById('offerModalSavings');\n  const codeEl         = document.getElementById('offerModalCode');\n  const specsEl        = document.getElementById('offerModalSpecs');\n  const inclusionsEl   = document.getElementById('offerModalInclusions');\n  const termsEl        = document.getElementById('offerModalTerms');\n\n  function closeModal() {\n    modalEl.classList.add('hidden');\n    document.body.style.overflow = '';\n  }\n\n  closeBtn?.addEventListener('click', closeModal);\n  dismissBtn?.addEventListener('click', closeModal);\n  modalEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === modalEl) closeModal(); });\n  document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {\n    if (e.key === 'Escape' && !modalEl.classList.contains('hidden')) closeModal();\n  });\n\n  return function openOfferModal(offerId) {\n    const offer = OFFER_VEHICLES.find((o) => o.id === offerId);\n    if (!offer) return;\n\n    const savings = offer.originalPrice - offer.finalPrice;\n\n    titleEl.textContent          = offer.name;\n    categoryEl.textContent       = offer.category;\n    badgeEl.textContent          = `\\u2212${offer.discountPercent}% OFF`;\n    imgEl.src                    = offer.image;\n    imgEl.alt                    = offer.alt;\n    attachImageFallback(imgEl, buildFallbackCarImage(offer.name));\n    summaryEl.textContent        = offer.summary;\n    originalPriceEl.textContent  = `Was: ${formatCurrency(offer.originalPrice)}`;\n    discountedPriceEl.innerHTML  = `${formatCurrency(offer.finalPrice)}<small> / sale price</small>`;\n    savingsEl.textContent        = `You save ${formatCurrency(savings)}`;\n    codeEl.textContent           = offer.promoCode;\n    specsEl.textContent          = offer.specs.join(' · ');\n    termsEl.textContent          = offer.terms;\n    inclusionsEl.innerHTML       = offer.inclusions\n      .map((item) => `<li>${escapeHtml(item)}</li>`)\n      .join('');\n\n    modalEl.classList.remove('hidden');\n    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';\n    closeBtn?.focus();\n  };\n}\n\n/* ── 7. PROMO CODE BOX ────────────────────────────────────── */\nfunction initPromoCodeBox() {\n  const form       = document.getElementById('promo-code-form');\n  const input      = document.getElementById('promo-code-input');\n  const feedbackEl = document.getElementById('promo-feedback');\n  if (!form || !input || !feedbackEl) return;\n\n  function showFeedback(type, message, activeCode = '') {\n    feedbackEl.className = `ov-notice ${type}`;\n\n    if (type === 'success' && activeCode) {\n      const removeId = 'ov-remove-promo';\n      feedbackEl.innerHTML = `\n        <span>${escapeHtml(message)}</span>\n        <button type=\"button\" class=\"ov-notice-remove\" id=\"${removeId}\">Remove</button>\n      `;\n      document.getElementById(removeId)?.addEventListener('click', () => {\n        localStorage.removeItem(PROMO_STORAGE_KEY);\n        input.value       = '';\n        feedbackEl.className = 'hidden';\n        feedbackEl.innerHTML = '';\n        input.focus();\n      });\n    } else {\n      feedbackEl.textContent = message;\n    }\n  }\n\n  function applyPromoCode(rawCode) {\n    const code = rawCode.trim().toUpperCase();\n    if (!code) {\n      showFeedback('error', 'Please enter a promo code before applying.');\n      return;\n    }\n    const promo = PROMO_CODES[code];\n    if (!promo) {\n      showFeedback('error', `\"${code}\" is not a valid code. Try VAULT15, STUDENT10, or WEEKEND20.`);\n      return;\n    }\n    localStorage.setItem(PROMO_STORAGE_KEY, code);\n    input.value = code;\n    showFeedback('success', `✓ Applied ${code}: ${promo.description} — saved for your session.`, code);\n  }\n\n  /* Restore saved code on page load */\n  const savedCode = (localStorage.getItem(PROMO_STORAGE_KEY) || '').trim().toUpperCase();\n  if (savedCode && PROMO_CODES[savedCode]) {\n    input.value = savedCode;\n    showFeedback(\n      'success',\n      `✓ Active code ${savedCode}: ${PROMO_CODES[savedCode].description} (restored from session).`,\n      savedCode\n    );\n  }\n\n  form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    applyPromoCode(input.value);\n  });\n}\n\n/* ── 8. DISCOUNT CALCULATOR ───────────────────────────────── */\nfunction initDiscountCalculator() {\n  const priceInput    = document.getElementById('calc-price');\n  const discountInput = document.getElementById('calc-discount');\n  const totalEl       = document.getElementById('calc-total');\n  const savingsEl     = document.getElementById('calc-savings');\n  const feedbackEl    = document.getElementById('calc-feedback');\n  if (!priceInput || !discountInput || !totalEl || !savingsEl || !feedbackEl) return;\n\n  function setError(msg) {\n    feedbackEl.textContent = msg;\n    totalEl.textContent    = formatCurrency(0);\n    savingsEl.textContent  = 'Savings: $0.00';\n  }\n\n  function recalculate() {\n    const rawPrice    = priceInput.value.trim();\n    const rawDiscount = discountInput.value.trim();\n\n    if (priceInput.validity?.badInput) { setError('Price must be a valid non-negative number.'); return; }\n    if (discountInput.validity?.badInput) { setError('Discount must be between 0 and 100.'); return; }\n\n    if (rawPrice === '' && rawDiscount === '') {\n      feedbackEl.textContent = 'Enter a price and discount percentage to see your live total.';\n      totalEl.textContent    = '$0.00';\n      savingsEl.textContent  = 'Savings: $0.00';\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (rawPrice === '') {\n      feedbackEl.textContent = 'Enter a price to calculate your discounted total.';\n      totalEl.textContent    = '$0.00';\n      savingsEl.textContent  = 'Savings: $0.00';\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const price    = Number(rawPrice);\n    const discount = rawDiscount === '' ? 0 : Number(rawDiscount);\n\n    if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price < 0) { setError('Price must be a valid non-negative number.'); return; }\n    if (rawDiscount !== '' && (!Number.isFinite(discount) || discount < 0 || discount > 100)) {\n      setError('Discount percentage must be between 0% and 100%.');\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const savings    = price * (discount / 100);\n    const finalTotal = Math.max(0, price - savings);\n\n    totalEl.textContent   = formatCurrency(finalTotal);\n    savingsEl.textContent = `Savings: ${formatCurrency(savings)}`;\n    feedbackEl.textContent = discount > 0\n      ? `${discount}% discount applied to ${formatCurrency(price)}.`\n      : 'Showing standard rate (0% discount).';\n  }\n\n  priceInput.addEventListener('input', recalculate);\n  discountInput.addEventListener('input', recalculate);\n  recalculate();\n}\n\n/* ── 9. INIT ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */\ndocument.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {\n  initMobileNav();\n  const openOfferModal = createOfferModalHandler();\n  renderOfferCards(openOfferModal);\n  initPromoCodeBox();\n  initDiscountCalculator();\n});\n","Overwrite":true,"Path":"C:\\Users\\ArcTech Systems\\Desktop\\SE-B_Group01_CarVault\\js\\offers.js"}}]}

Here's a full summary of what was built:\n\n---\n\n## ✅ Files Updated\n\n| File | Status |\n|------|--------|\n| [`pages/offers.html`](file:///C:/Users/ArcTech%20Systems/Desktop/SE-B_Group01_CarVault/pages/offers.html) | Fully rebuilt (353 lines) |\n| [`css/offers-theme.css`](file:///C:/Users/ArcTech%20Systems/Desktop/SE-B_Group01_CarVault/css/offers-theme.css) | Created (895 lines) |\n| [`js/offers.js`](file:///C:/Users/ArcTech%20Systems/Desktop/SE-B_Group01_CarVault/js/offers.js) | Fully rebuilt (395 lines) |\n\n---\n\n## What was implemented\n\n### 🏷️ Topbar\nDark slate `#1d1f21` · \"THE ART OF AUTOMOTIVE EXCELLENCE · ISLAMABAD, PAKISTAN\"\n\n### 🔗 Header & Logo\n- Dark obsidian `#141517` sticky header\n- **CAR ✦ VAULT** logo with gold `#eab308` star\n- **12 nav links**: HOME, EXPLORE CARS, BRANDS, NEW ARRIVALS, **OFFERS** (active), ABOUT, CONTACT, COMPARE, FINANCE, TEST DRIVE, SAVED CARS, ♡ ADMIN\n- Rounded gold pill CTA `BOOK A TEST DRIVE →` → `test-drive.html`\n\n### 🌑 Hero (dark charcoal `#16181b → #1d1f23`)\n- Gold eyebrow: **CARVAULT / SEASONAL PRIVILEGES**\n- Plus Jakarta Sans white heading: **Exclusive Offers.**\n- Muted subtitle + two CTA buttons\n\n### 🃏 3 Offer Cards (white `#ffffff` body)\nExact cars, prices, Unsplash images, promo codes, and `onerror` SVG fallbacks — all on crisp white cards with gold `#eab308` accents and light `#e2e6ec` borders\n\n### 🛠️ Interactive Tools\n- **Promo code validator** — validates VAULT15/STUDENT10/WEEKEND20, persists to `localStorage`, shows success/error notices with a Remove button\n- **Live savings calculator** — real-time input, computes discount and shows formatted total + savings\n\n### 🪟 Modal\nFully working View Details modal — white dialog, dark glass backdrop, price breakdown, inclusions list, terms box, \"Book a Test Drive →\" CTA\n\n### 🦶 Footer\nDark `#141517` footer matching the header, with 4-column grid, **CAR ✦ VAULT** logo, and correct copyright line"}

);\\n344:   }\\n345: \\n346:   const savedCode = (localStorage.getItem(PROMO_STORAGE_KEY) || '').trim().toUpperCase();\\n347:   if (savedCode && PROMO_CODES[savedCode]) {\\n348:     const promo = applyPromoCode(input.value);\\n360:   });\\n361: }\\n362: \\n363: /**\\n364:  * Initializes the real-time Discount Calculator Box.\\n365:  */\\n366: function initDiscountCalculator() {\\n367:   const const savingsEl = document.getElementById('calc-savings');\\n371:   const feedbackEl = document.getElementById('calc-feedback');\\n372:   if (!priceInput || !discountInput || !totalEl || !savingsEl || return;\\n393:     }\\n394: \\n395:     if (rawPrice === '' && rawDiscount === '') {\\n396:       feedbackEl.className = 'meta';\\n397:       feedbackEl.style.marginTop = '12px';\\n398:       feedbackEl.textContent =\\n399:         'Enter a rental price and discount percentage to see your live total.';\\n400:       totalEl.textContent = formatCurrency(0);\\n401:       savingsEl.textContent = `Savings: ${formatCurrency(0)}`;\\n402:       return;\\n403:     }\\n404: \\n405:     const price = rawPrice === '' ? NaN : Number(rawPrice);\\n406:     const discount = rawDiscount === '' ? 0 : Number(rawDiscount);\\n407: \\n408:     if (rawPrice === '') {\\n409:       feedbackEl.className = 'meta';\\n410:       feedbackEl.style.marginTop = '12px';\\n411:       feedbackEl.textContent = 'Enter a rental price to calculate your discounted total.';\\n412:       totalEl.textContent = formatCurrency(0);\\n413: non-negative amount.');\\n419:       return;\\n420:     }\\n421: \\n422:     if (rawDiscount !== '' && (!Number.isFinite(discount) || discount < 0 || discount > 100)) {\\n423:       setError('Discount percentage must be between 0% and 100%.');\\n424:       return;\\n425:     }\\n426: \\n427:     const savings = price * (discount / 100);\\n428:     const finalTotal = Math.max(0, price - ${formatCurrency(price)}.`\\n437:         : 'Showing standard rate (0% discount).';\\n438:   }\\n439: \\n440:   priceInput.addEventListener('input', updateCalculation);\\n441: DESIGN TOKENS ───────────────────────────────────────── */\\n:root {\\n  /* Topbar / Header */\\n  --ov-topbar-bg   : #1d1f21;\\n  --ov-header-bg   : #141517;\\n\\n  /* Gold */\\n  --ov-gold        : #eab308;\\n  --ov-gold-hover  : #fbbf24;\\n\\n  /* Body (white theme) */\\n  --ov-body-bg     : #ffffff;\\n  --ov-body-alt    : #f8f9fa;\\n  --ov-text        : #111315;\\n  --ov-muted       : #5a6372;\\n  --ov-border      : #e2e6ec;\\n\\n  /* Hero (dark charcoal) */\\n  --ov-hero-from   : #16181b;\\n  --ov-hero-to     : #1d1f23;\\n\\n  /* Footer */\\n  --ov-footer-bg   : #141517;\\n  --ov-footer-line : #2a2c30;\\n}\\n\\n/* ── 2. BASE BODY RESET for white theme ─────────────────────── */\\nbody {\\n  background : var(--ov-body-bg);\\n  color      : var(--ov-text);\\n}\\n\\n/* ── 3. TOPBAR ───────────────────────────────────────────────── */\\n.topbar {\\n  background    : var(--ov-topbar-bg) !important;\\n  color         : #9ca3af !important;\\n  font-size     : 11px;\\n  letter-spacing: .18em;\\n  padding       : 9px 16px;\\n  text-align    : center;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  border-bottom : 1px solid #2a2c30;\\n}\\n\\n/* ── 4. HEADER & NAV ─────────────────────────────────────────── */\\n.header {\\n  position      : sticky;\\n  top           : 0;\\n  z-index       : 50;\\n  background    : var(--ov-header-bg) !important;\\n  backdrop-filter: none;\\n  border-bottom : 1px solid #2a2c30;\\n}\\n\\n/* Logo */\\n.ov-logo {\\n  font-family   : 'Plus Jakarta Sans', 'Manrope', sans-serif;\\n  font-size     : 20px;\\n  font-weight   : 800;\\n  letter-spacing: .18em;\\n  color         : #f1f5f9;\\n  text-decoration: none;\\n  display       : inline-flex;\\n  align-items   : center;\\n  gap           : 2px;\\n  white-space   : nowrap;\\n}\\n.ov-logo .ov-star {\\n  color : var(--ov-gold);\\n  font-size: 14px;\\n  margin: 0 3px;\\n  line-height: 1;\\n}\\n\\n/* Nav links */\\n.navlinks a {\\n  color         : #cbd5e1 !important;\\n  font-size     : 11px;\\n  letter-spacing: .11em;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  transition    : color .18s;\\n}\\n.navlinks a:hover,\\n.navlinks a.active {\\n  color : var(--ov-gold) !important;\\n}\\n\\n/* CTA pill button */\\n.ov-cta-btn {\\n  display       : inline-flex;\\n  align-items   : center;\\n  gap           : 6px;\\n  padding       : 10px 20px;\\n  background    : var(--ov-gold);\\n  color         : #111315 !important;\\n  border        : none;\\n  border-radius : 999px;\\n  font-size     : 11px;\\n  font-weight   : 800;\\n  letter-spacing: .09em;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  text-decoration: none;\\n  white-space   : nowrap;\\n  transition    : background .18s, transform .18s;\\n  cursor        : pointer;\\n}\\n.ov-cta-btn:hover {\\n  background : var(--ov-gold-hover);\\n  transform  : translateY(-2px);\\n  color      : #111315 !important;\\n}\\n\\n/* Mobile menu toggle */\\n#menuToggle {\\n  background    : transparent;\\n  border        : 1px solid #3a3d42;\\n  color         : #cbd5e1;\\n  border-radius : 6px;\\n  padding       : 8px 14px;\\n  font-size     : 11px;\\n  letter-spacing: .1em;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n}\\n#menuToggle:hover {\\n  border-color  : var(--ov-gold);\\n  color         : var(--ov-gold);\\n}\\n\\n@media (max-width: 960px) {\\n  .navlinks {\\n    background    : #1a1c1f !important;\\n    border-bottom : 1px solid #2a2c30 !important;\\n    top           : 70px !important;\\n  }\\n}\\n\\n/* ── 5. HERO (dark charcoal) ─────────────────────────────────── */\\n.ov-hero {\\n  min-height    : 480px;\\n  display       : flex;\\n  align-items   : center;\\n  background    : linear-gradient(135deg, var(--ov-hero-from) 0%, var(--ov-hero-to) 100%);\\n  padding       : 90px 0 80px;\\n  position      : relative;\\n  overflow      : hidden;\\n}\\n.ov-hero::before {\\n  content    : '';\\n  position   : absolute;\\n  inset      : 0;\\n  background : radial-gradient(ellipse at 80% 30%, rgba(234,179,8,.07) 0%, transparent 65%),\\n               radial-gradient(ellipse at 10% 70%, rgba(255,255,255,.03) 0%, transparent 55%);\\n  pointer-events: none;\\n}\\n.ov-hero .wrap { position: relative; z-index: 1; }\\n\\n.ov-hero-eyebrow {\\n  color         : var(--ov-gold);\\n  font-size     : 11px;\\n  font-weight   : 800;\\n  letter-spacing: .3em;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  margin-bottom : 18px;\\n}\\n.ov-hero h1 {\\n  font-family   : 'Plus Jakarta Sans', 'Manrope', sans-serif;\\n  font-size     : clamp(2.6rem, 5vw, 4.4rem);\\n  font-weight   : 800;\\n  color         : #f1f5f9;\\n  letter-spacing: -.045em;\\n  line-height   : 1.08;\\n  margin-bottom : 18px;\\n  max-width     : 680px;\\n}\\n.ov-hero p {\\n  color         : #9ca3af;\\n  font-size     : 16px;\\n  max-width     : 520px;\\n  margin-bottom : 34px;\\n  line-height   : 1.7;\\n}\\n.ov-hero-actions {\\n  display       : flex;\\n  gap           : 12px;\\n  flex-wrap     : wrap;\\n}\\n.ov-hero-btn-primary {\\n  display       : inline-flex;\\n  align-items   : center;\\n  gap           : 7px;\\n  padding       : 13px 26px;\\n  background    : var(--ov-gold);\\n  color         : #111315 !important;\\n  border-radius : 8px;\\n  font-size     : 12px;\\n  font-weight   : 800;\\n  letter-spacing: .08em;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  text-decoration: none;\\n  transition    : background .18s, transform .18s;\\n  border        : none;\\n}\\n.ov-hero-btn-primary:hover {\\n  background : var(--ov-gold-hover);\\n  transform  : translateY(-2px);\\n}\\n.ov-hero-btn-outline {\\n  display       : inline-flex;\\n  align-items   : center;\\n  gap           : 7px;\\n  padding       : 13px 26px;\\n  background    : transparent;\\n  color         : #f1f5f9 !important;\\n  border        : 1px solid #3a3d42;\\n  border-radius : 8px;\\n  font-size     : 12px;\\n  font-weight   : 700;\\n  letter-spacing: .08em;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  text-decoration: none;\\n  transition    : border-color .18s, color .18s;\\n}\\n.ov-hero-btn-outline:hover {\\n  border-color : var(--ov-gold);\\n  color        : var(--ov-gold) !important;\\n}\\n\\n/* ── 6. MAIN BODY — white sections ───────────────────────────── */\\nmain {\\n  background : var(--ov-body-bg);\\n}\\n\\n/* Regular sections */\\n.ov-section {\\n  padding    : 80px 0;\\n  background : var(--ov-body-bg);\\n}\\n.ov-section-alt {\\n  padding    : 80px 0;\\n  background : var(--ov-body-alt);\\n}\\n\\n/* Section header */\\n.ov-section-head {\\n  display        : flex;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  align-items    : flex-end;\\n  gap            : 24px;\\n  margin-bottom  : 36px;\\n}\\n.ov-section-head p {\\n  max-width : 440px;\\n  color     : var(--ov-muted);\\n  font-size : 14px;\\n  line-height: 1.65;\\n  margin    : 0;\\n}\\n\\n.ov-eyebrow {\\n  color         : var(--ov-gold);\\n  font-size     : 10px;\\n  font-weight   : 800;\\n  letter-spacing: .28em;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  margin-bottom : 10px;\\n}\\n.ov-heading {\\n  font-family   : 'Plus Jakarta Sans', 'Manrope', sans-serif;\\n  font-size     : clamp(1.6rem, 2.8vw, 2.4rem);\\n  font-weight   : 800;\\n  color         : var(--ov-text);\\n  letter-spacing: -.03em;\\n  margin        : 0;\\n}\\n\\n/* ── 7. OFFER CARDS (white body) ─────────────────────────────── */\\n.ov-grid {\\n  display              : grid;\\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));\\n  gap                  : 24px;\\n}\\n\\n.ov-card {\\n  background    : #ffffff;\\n  border        : 1px solid var(--ov-border);\\n  border-radius : 12px;\\n  overflow      : hidden;\\n  transition    : border-color .22s, box-shadow .22s, transform .22s;\\n}\\n.ov-card:hover {\\n  border-color : var(--ov-gold);\\n  box-shadow   : 0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.10);\\n  transform    : 100%;\\n  height     : 100%;\\n  object-fit : cover;\\n  display    : block;\\n  transition : transform .45s;\\n}\\n.ov-card:hover .ov-card-img-wrap img {\\n  transform  : scale(1.045);\\n}\\n\\n.ov-badge {\\n  position      : absolute;\\n  top           : 14px;\\n  left          : 14px;\\n  padding       : 5px 10px;\\n  background    : var(--ov-gold);\\n  color 24px;\\n}\\n.ov-card-category {\\n  font-size     : 11px;\\n  font-weight   : 700;\\n  color         : var(--ov-muted);\\n  letter-spacing: .07em;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  margin-bottom : }\\n\\n.ov-apply-btn {\\n  flex-shrink   : 0;\\n  padding       : 12px 20px;\\n  background    : var(--ov-gold);\\n  color         : #111315;\\n  border        : none;\\n  border-radius : 8px;\\n  font-size     : 12px;\\n  font-weight   : 800;\\n  letter-spacing: .08em;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  cursor        : pointer;\\n  transition    : background .18s;\\n  white-space   : nowrap;\\n}\\n.ov-apply-btn:hover { background: var(--ov-gold-hover); }\\n\\n.ov-active-codes {\\n  margin-top  : 16px;\\n  font-size   : 12px;\\n  color       : var(--ov-muted);\\n}\\n.ov-active-codes .ov-code-chip {\\n  display       : inline-flex;\\n  align-items   : center;\\n  gap           : 3px;\\n  background    : #fefce8;\\n  border        : 1px solid #fde68a;\\n  border-radius : 4px;\\n  padding       : 2px 7px;\\n  font-size     : 11px;\\n  font-weight   : 700;\\n  color         : #92400e;\\n  margin        : 0 3px;\\n}\\n\\n/* Promo feedback notices */\\n.ov-notice {\\n  margin-top    : 14px;\\n  padding       : 12px 16px;\\n  border-radius : 8px;\\n  font-size     : 13px;\\n  line-height   : 1.5;\\n  display       : flex;\\n  align-items   : center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap           : 10px;\\n}\\n.ov-notice.success {\\n  background : #f0fdf4;\\n  border     : 1px solid #bbf7d0;\\n  color      : #166534;\\n}\\n.ov-notice.error {\\n  background : #fef2f2;\\n  border     : 1px solid #fecaca;\\n  color      : #991b1b;\\n}\\n.ov-notice-remove {\\n  flex-shrink   : 0;\\n  padding       : 4px 10px;\\n  background    : transparent;\\n  border        : 1px solid currentColor;\\n  border-radius : 6px;\\n  font-size     : 11px;\\n  font-weight   : 700;\\n  cursor        : pointer;\\n  color         : inherit;\\n  opacity       : .75;\\n  transition    : opacity .15s;\\n}\\n.ov-notice-remove:hover { opacity: 1; FALLBACK SVG BUILDER ──────────────────────────────── */\\nfunction buildFallbackCarImage(label) {\\n  const svg = `\\n    <svg xmlns=\\\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 900 540\\\" role=\\\"img\\\" aria-label=\\\"${label}\\\">\\n      <defs>\\n        <linearGradient id=\\\"cvbg\\\" x1=\\\"0\\\" y1=\\\"0\\\" x2=\\\"1\\\" y2=\\\"1\\\">\\n          <stop offset=\\\"0%\\\" stop-color=\\\"#16181b\\\"/>\\n          <stop offset=\\\"100%\\\" stop-color=\\\"#1d1f23\\\"/>\\n        </linearGradient>\\n      </defs>\\n      <rect width=\\\"900\\\" height=\\\"540\\\" fill=\\\"url(#cvbg)\\\"/>\\n      <circle cx=\\\"720\\\" cy=\\\"110\\\" r=\\\"160\\\" fill=\\\"#eab308\\\" opacity=\\\"0.08\\\"/>\\n      <path d=\\\"M180 340 L240 250 Q285 200 375 198 L545 198 Q620 202 675 254 L760 272 Q780 286 774 340 Z\\\"\\n            fill=\\\"#23262a\\\" stroke=\\\"#eab308\\\" stroke-width=\\\"3\\\"/>\\n      <circle cx=\\\"305\\\" cy=\\\"342\\\" r=\\\"44\\\" fill=\\\"#16181b\\\" stroke=\\\"#eab308\\\" stroke-width=\\\"5\\\"/>\\n      <circle cx=\\\"620\\\" cy=\\\"342\\\" r=\\\"44\\\" fill=\\\"#16181b\\\" stroke=\\\"#eab308\\\" stroke-width=\\\"5\\\"/>\\n      <text x=\\\"56\\"}
